 283: const Store = {
 284:   key:'kidmath_v1',
 285:   data:null,
 286:   defaults(){
 287:     const sk = () => ({lvl:1, up:0, down:0, wins:0, tries:0});
 288:     return {v:1, stars:0, diff:'auto', voiceURI:null, rot:{}, irot:{}, seen:{}, calm:false, keepAlive:true,
 289:       skills:{count:sk(), numeral:sk(), produce:sk(), flash:sk(), compare:sk(), compose:sk(), add:sk()}};
 290:   },
 291:   load(){
 292:     let d = null;
 293:     try{ d = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(this.key) || 'null'); }catch(e){ d = null; }
 294:     const base = this.defaults();
 295:     if(d && d.v === 1 && d.skills){
 296:       for(const k in base.skills){ base.skills[k] = Object.assign(base.skills[k], d.skills[k] || {}); }
 297:       base.stars = (typeof d.stars === 'number' && d.stars >= 0) ? d.stars : 0;
 298:       base.diff = ['auto','1','2','3'].includes(d.diff) ? d.diff : 'auto';
 299:       base.voiceURI = (typeof d.voiceURI === 'string') ? d.voiceURI : null;
 300:       // Ã§Â»â€œÃ¦Å¾â€žÃ¦Â Â¡Ã©ÂªÅ’Ã¯Â¼Ë†P2-02Ã¯Â¼â€°: rot/irot Ã¤Â»â€¦Ã¦Å½Â¥Ã¥Ââ€”Ã¦â€¢Â°Ã¥â‚¬Â¼Ã¨Â¡Â¨, seen Ã¤Â»â€¦Ã¦Å½Â¥Ã¥Ââ€”Ã¥Â¸Æ’Ã¥Â°â€Ã§Å“Å¸Ã¥â‚¬Â¼Ã¨Â¡Â¨
 301:       const numMap = o => { const r = {};
 302:         if(o && typeof o === 'object' && !Array.isArray(o)) for(const k in o){ if(typeof o[k] === 'number' && isFinite(o[k])) r[k] = o[k]; }
 303:         return r; };
 304:       base.rot = numMap(d.rot); base.irot = numMap(d.irot);
 305:       const seen = {};
 306:       if(d.seen && typeof d.seen === 'object' && !Array.isArray(d.seen)) for(const k in d.seen){ if(d.seen[k] === true) seen[k] = true; }
 307:       base.seen = seen;
 308:       base.calm = d.calm === true;
 309:       base.keepAlive = d.keepAlive !== false;
 310:     }
 311:     this.data = base;
 312:   },

 358:   pickVoice(){

 543: /* ============ 5. SfxÃ¯Â¼Ë†WebAudio Ã¥ÂË†Ã¦Ë†ÂÃ©Å¸Â³Ã¦â€¢Ë†Ã¯Â¼â€° ============ */
 544: const Sfx = {
 545:   ctx:null, master:null, silentEl:null,
 546:   ensure(){
 547:     try{
 548:       const AC = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
 549:       if(!AC) return;
 550:       if(!this.ctx){
 551:         this.ctx = new AC();
 552:         this.master = this.ctx.createGain(); this.master.gain.value = .5;
 553:         this.master.connect(this.ctx.destination);
 554:       }
 555:       // iOS 'interrupted'Ã¯Â¼Ë†Ã§â€ÂµÃ¨Â¯Â/SiriÃ¯Â¼â€°Ã¤Â¸Å½ 'suspended' Ã©Æ’Â½Ã¥Å“Â¨Ã¦â€°â€¹Ã¥Å Â¿Ã¥â€ â€¦Ã¦ÂÂ¢Ã¥Â¤ÂÃ¯Â¼Ë†I10Ã¯Â¼â€°
 556:       if(this.ctx.state !== 'running'){ const p = this.ctx.resume(); if(p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {}); }
 557:       // pagehide Ã¦Å¡â€šÃ¥ÂÅ“Ã¨Â¿â€¡Ã§Å¡â€žÃ¦â€”Â Ã¥Â£Â°Ã¤Â¿ÂÃ¦Â´Â» loop Ã¥Å“Â¨Ã¤Â¸â€¹Ã¤Â¸â‚¬Ã¦Â¬Â¡Ã¦â€°â€¹Ã¥Å Â¿Ã¥â€ â€¦Ã¦ÂÂ¢Ã¥Â¤Â
 558:       if(this.silentEl && this.silentEl.paused && !document.hidden && Store.data.keepAlive){
 559:         const p = this.silentEl.play(); if(p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {});
 560:       }
 561:     }catch(e){}
 562:   },
 563:   duck(on){ try{ if(this.master) this.master.gain.value = on ? .16 : .5; }catch(e){} },
 564:   unlock(){
 565:     this.ensure();
 566:     try{ const b = this.ctx.createBuffer(1, 1, 22050); const s = this.ctx.createBufferSource();
 567:       s.buffer = b; s.connect(this.ctx.destination); s.start(0); }catch(e){}
 568:     // Ã¥â€ â€ºÃ¨Â§â€ž5: Ã¥Â¾ÂªÃ§Å½Â¯Ã¦â€”Â Ã¥Â£Â° <audio> Ã¦ÂÂÃ¥Ââ€¡Ã¥Âªâ€™Ã¤Â½â€œÃ¤Â¼Å¡Ã¨Â¯ÂÃ¯Â¼Ë†Ã§Â»ÂÃ©ÂªÅ’Ã¦â‚¬Â§ workaround, Ã©ÂÅ¾Ã¥Â¹Â³Ã¥ÂÂ°Ã¤Â¿ÂÃ¨Â¯Â; Ã¥Â®Â¶Ã©â€¢Â¿Ã©ÂÂ¢Ã¦ÂÂ¿Ã¥ÂÂ¯Ã¥â€¦Â³Ã¯Â¼â€°
 569:     try{
 570:       if(!Store.data.keepAlive) return;
 571:       if(!this.silentEl){
 572:         const a = document.createElement('audio');
 573:         a.setAttribute('playsinline',''); a.loop = true; a.volume = .01;
 574:         a.src = this._silentWav(); document.body.appendChild(a); this.silentEl = a;
 575:         // Ã§â€Å¸Ã¥â€˜Â½Ã¥â€˜Â¨Ã¦Å“Å¸: Ã©Å¡ÂÃ¨â€”Â/Ã§Â¦Â»Ã¥Â¼â‚¬Ã©Â¡ÂµÃ©ÂÂ¢Ã¦ËœÂ¾Ã¥Â¼ÂÃ¦Å¡â€šÃ¥ÂÅ“; Ã¥â€ºÅ¾Ã¥Ë†Â°Ã¥ÂÂ¯Ã¨Â§ÂÃ¥Â°ÂÃ¨Â¯â€¢Ã¦ÂÂ¢Ã¥Â¤Â(Ã¨Â¢Â«Ã¦â€¹â€™Ã¥Ë†â„¢Ã§Â­â€°Ã¤Â¸â€¹Ã¤Â¸â‚¬Ã¦Â¬Â¡Ã¦â€°â€¹Ã¥Å Â¿Ã¥â€ â€¦ unlock)
 576:         document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
 577:           if(!this.silentEl) return;
 578:           if(document.hidden){ try{ this.silentEl.pause(); }catch(e){} }
 579:           else { try{ const p = this.silentEl.play(); if(p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {}); }catch(e){} }
 580:         });
 581:         window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => { try{ this.silentEl && this.silentEl.pause(); }catch(e){} });
 582:       }
 583:       const p = this.silentEl.play(); if(p && p.catch) p.catch(() => {});
 584:     }catch(e){}
 585:   },
 586:   stopKeepAlive(){ try{ if(this.silentEl){ this.silentEl.pause(); this.silentEl.remove(); this.silentEl = null; } }catch(e){} },
 587:   _silentWav(){
 588:     const sr = 8000, n = 4000, size = 44 + n, b = new Uint8Array(size), dv = new DataView(b.buffer);
 589:     const ws = (o, s) => { for(let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) b[o + i] = s.charCodeAt(i); };
 590:     ws(0,'RIFF'); dv.setUint32(4, 36 + n, true); ws(8,'WAVE'); ws(12,'fmt ');
 591:     dv.setUint32(16,16,true); dv.setUint16(20,1,true); dv.setUint16(22,1,true);
 592:     dv.setUint32(24,sr,true); dv.setUint32(28,sr,true); dv.setUint16(32,1,true); dv.setUint16(34,8,true);
 593:     ws(36,'data'); dv.setUint32(40,n,true); b.fill(128,44);
 594:     let s = ''; for(let i = 0; i < size; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
 595:     return 'data:audio/wav;base64,' + btoa(s);
 596:   },
 597:   tone(f0, f1, dur, type, vol, when){
 598:     try{
 599:       if(!this.ctx) return;
 600:       const t = this.ctx.currentTime + (when || 0);
 601:       const o = this.ctx.createOscillator(), g = this.ctx.createGain();
 602:       o.type = type || 'sine'; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t);
 603:       if(f1) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(f1, t + dur);
 604:       g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001, t);
 605:       g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol || .3, t + .015);
 606:       g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001, t + dur);
 607:       o.connect(g); g.connect(this.master); o.start(t); o.stop(t + dur + .06);
 608:     }catch(e){}
 609:   },
 610:   pop(){ this.ensure(); this.tone(520, 900, .09, 'triangle', .45) },
 611:   ding(){ this.ensure(); this.tone(880,0,.12,'sine',.32); this.tone(1174.7,0,.14,'sine',.28,.07); this.tone(1568,0,.24,'sine',.28,.14) },
 612:   chomp(){ this.ensure(); this.tone(230,90,.09,'square',.32); this.tone(150,70,.11,'square',.26,.06) },
 613:   soft(){ this.ensure(); this.tone(392,330,.28,'sine',.2) },
 614:   yay(){ this.ensure(); [523.25,659.25,783.99,1046.5,1318.5].forEach((f,i)=>this.tone(f,0,.16,'triangle',.28,i*.085)) },
 615:   woosh(){ this.ensure(); this.tone(1300,320,.24,'sawtooth',.1) },
 616:   fly(){ this.ensure(); this.tone(500,1500,.4,'sine',.22) }
 617: };

 723: function scatterPositions(n){
 724:   const cols = n <= 3 ? n : n <= 6 ? 3 : n <= 8 ? 4 : 4;
 725:   const rows = Math.ceil(n / cols);
 726:   const cells = [];
 727:   for(let r = 0; r < rows; r++) for(let c = 0; c < cols; c++) cells.push({r, c});
 728:   return shuffle(cells).slice(0, n).map(({r, c}) => ({
 729:     x: (c + .5) / cols * 100 + (rng() * 8 - 4),
 730:     y: (r + .5) / rows * 100 + (rng() * 8 - 4)
 731:   }));
 732: }
 733: function distractors(correct, max){
 734:   const set = new Set([correct]);
 735:   const cand = shuffle([correct - 1, correct + 1, correct - 2, correct + 2, correct + 3]
 736:     .filter(v => v >= 1 && v <= Math.max(max, correct + 2) && v <= 10));
 737:   for(const v of cand){ if(set.size < 3) set.add(v); }
 738:   let guard = 20;
 739:   while(set.size < 3 && guard--){ const v = 1 + ri(10); set.add(v); }
 740:   return shuffle([...set]);
 741: }

  79: def state(page):

  88: def click_answer(page, answer, wrong_first=False):
  89:     page.wait_for_selector(".cards .ncard", timeout=12000)
  90:     if answer is None:
  91:         # count/add/compose/flash: data-answer Ã§â€Â± askNumber Ã¦Å’â€šÃ¨Â½Â½Ã¦â€”Â¶Ã¨Â®Â¾Ã§Â½Â®, Ã¥Â¿â€¦Ã©Â¡Â»Ã¥Å“Â¨Ã¥ÂÂ¡Ã§â€°â€¡Ã¥â€¡ÂºÃ§Å½Â°Ã¥ÂÅ½Ã¨Â¯Â»Ã¥Ââ€“
  92:         answer = page.evaluate("() => document.querySelector('#stage').dataset.answer || ''")

  94:             raise Fail("no data-answer after cards appeared")
  95:     if wrong_first:
  96:         wrongs = page.locator(f".cards .ncard:not([data-value='{answer}'])")

  98:             wrongs.first.click(timeout=3000)

 100:                 "() => { const b=document.querySelector('.cards'); return b && getComputedStyle(b).visibility!=='hidden'; }",
 101:                 timeout=15000)
 102:     # Ã©â€¡ÂÃ©â€”Â®Ã¨Â¯Â­Ã©Å¸Â³Ã¦Å“Å¸Ã©â€”Â´ lock Ã¦Å“ÂªÃ©â€¡Å Ã¦â€Â¾Ã¤Â¼Å¡Ã¥ÂÅ¾Ã§â€šÂ¹Ã¥â€¡Â» Ã¢â€ â€™ Ã¨Â½Â®Ã¨Â¯Â¢Ã§â€šÂ¹Ã¥â€¡Â»Ã§â€ºÂ´Ã¥Ë†Â°Ã¥ÂÂ¡Ã§â€°â€¡Ã¦Â¶Ë†Ã¥Â¤Â±Ã¯Â¼Ë†Ã¦Â´Â»Ã¥Å Â¨Ã¥Â®Å’Ã¦Ë†ÂÃ¯Â¼â€°
 103:     deadline = time.time() + 15

 117: def audit_sizes(page, label):
 118:     """Ã¦Â Â¸Ã¥Â¿Æ’Ã¨Â§Â¦Ã¦â€˜Â¸Ã§â€ºÂ®Ã¦Â â€¡ Ã¢â€°Â¥88pxÃ¯Â¼Ë†Ã¦ÂµÂ®Ã§â€šÂ¹Ã¥Â®Â¹Ã¥Â·Â® 87.5; Ã¦Â§Â½Ã¤Â½Â/Ã¥Â¾Â½Ã§Â«Â Ã§Â­â€°Ã©ÂÅ¾Ã§â€ºÂ®Ã¦Â â€¡Ã¥â€¦Æ’Ã§Â´Â Ã¤Â¸ÂÃ¥Å“Â¨Ã¥Ë†â€”Ã¯Â¼â€°"""
 119:     bad = page.evaluate("""() => {
 120:       const bad = [];
 121:       for(const sel of ['.countable','.ncard','.balloon','#btnHome','#btnReplay','#btnDone','.tray .item','.srcgrp .item']){

 132: def solve_current(page, wrong_first=False, slow_count=False, audit=None):
 133:     st = state(page)
 134:     act, ans = st["act"], st["answer"]
 135:     if audit:
 136:         audit_sizes(page, f"{audit}/{act}")
 137:     if act in ("count", "add"):
 138:         deadline = time.time() + 25
 139:         while time.time() < deadline:
 140:             loc = page.locator(".countable:not(.counted)")
 141:             if loc.count() == 0:
 142:                 break
 143:             loc.first.click(timeout=4000)
 144:             page.wait_for_timeout(700 if slow_count else 150)
 145:         click_answer(page, None, wrong_first)
 146:     elif act == "flash":
 147:         page.wait_for_selector(".flashcover", timeout=15000)
 148:         click_answer(page, None, wrong_first)
 149:     elif act == "numeral":
 150:         page.locator(f".balloon[data-value='{ans}']").click(timeout=8000)
 151:     elif act == "produce":
 152:         n = int(ans)
 153:         for _ in range(n):
 154:             page.locator(".tray .item:not(.dim)").first.click(timeout=4000)
 155:             page.wait_for_timeout(170)
 156:         page.locator("#btnDone").click(timeout=5000)
 157:     elif act == "compare":
 158:         idx = page.evaluate(f"""() => {{
 159:           const sides = [...document.querySelectorAll('.side')];
 160:           return sides.findIndex(s => s.dataset.char === '{ans}'); }}""")
 161:         COMPARE_SIDES.append('L' if idx == 0 else 'R')
 162:         page.locator(f".side[data-char='{ans}']").click(timeout=5000)
 163:     elif act == "compose":
 164:         deadline = time.time() + 25
 165:         while time.time() < deadline:
 166:             loc = page.locator(".srcgrp .item:not(.dim)")
 167:             if loc.count() == 0:
 168:                 break
 169:             loc.first.click(timeout=4000)
 170:             page.wait_for_timeout(150)
 171:         click_answer(page, None, wrong_first)
 172:     else:
 173:         raise Fail(f"unknown activity {act}")
 174:     return act
 175: 
 176: def wait_new_activity(page, prev_aid, timeout=30):
 177:     deadline = time.time() + timeout
 178:     while time.time() < deadline:
 179:         st = state(page)
 180:         if st["cele"]:
 181:             return "cele"
 182:         # Ã¥Â¿â€¦Ã©Â¡Â»Ã¥Å“Â¨Ã¦Â¸Â¸Ã¦Ë†ÂÃ¥Å“ÂºÃ¦â„¢Â¯Ã¤Â¸Â­Ã¤Â¸â€Ã¦Â´Â»Ã¥Å Â¨Ã¥Â·Â²Ã¦Å’â€šÃ¨Â½Â½Ã¯Â¼Ë†Ã©ÂÂ¿Ã¥â€¦ÂÃ¨Â¯Â»Ã¥Ë†Â°Ã¨Â¿â€¡Ã¦Â¸Â¡Ã¦Å“Å¸Ã§Å¡â€žÃ©â„¢Ë†Ã¦â€”Â§ datasetÃ¯Â¼â€°
 183:         if st["scene"] == "scenePlay" and st["act"] and st["aid"] != prev_aid:
 184:             return st["aid"]
 185:         page.wait_for_timeout(120)
 186:     raise Fail(f"no new activity after aid={prev_aid}")
 187: 
 188: def play_island(page, idx, types_seen, shots_prefix=None, wrong_on_first=False, slow_first_count=False, audit=None):
 189:     page.locator(".island").nth(idx).click(timeout=5000)
 190:     aid = wait_new_activity(page, -999)
 191:     n_done = 0
 192:     first = True
 193:     while True:
 194:         st = state(page)
 195:         if st["cele"]:
 196:             if shots_prefix:
 197:                 page.screenshot(path=os.path.join(SHOTS, f"{shots_prefix}_cele.png"))
 198:             page.locator("#cele").click(timeout=4000)
 199:             page.wait_for_function("() => document.body.dataset.scene === 'sceneMap'", timeout=15000)
 200:             break
 201:         page.wait_for_timeout(250)
 202:         act = st["act"]
 203:         if shots_prefix and act not in types_seen:
 204:             page.wait_for_timeout(400)
 205:             page.screenshot(path=os.path.join(SHOTS, f"{shots_prefix}_{act}.png"))
 206:         solved = solve_current(page, wrong_first=(wrong_on_first and first),
 207:                                slow_count=(slow_first_count and first and act == "count"),
 208:                                audit=audit)
 209:         types_seen.add(solved)
 210:         n_done += 1
 211:         first = False
 212:         try:
 213:             nxt = wait_new_activity(page, st["aid"])
 214:         except Fail:
 215:             if state(page)["scene"] == "sceneMap":
 216:                 break
 217:             raise
 218:         if nxt == "cele":
 219:             continue
 220:         if n_done > 8:
 221:             raise Fail("island did not finish in 8 activities")
 222:     return n_done

 224: def get_speech(page):
 225:     return page.evaluate("window.__SPEECH.slice()")
 226: 
 227: def assert_speech_rules(log, label):
 228:     speaks = [e for e in log if e["a"] == "speak"]
 229:     welcome = [s for s in speaks if "Ã¦Â¬Â¢Ã¨Â¿Å½Ã¦ÂÂ¥Ã¥Ë†Â°Ã¦â€¢Â°Ã¥Â­â€”Ã¥Â°ÂÃ¨â€¹Â±Ã©â€ºâ€žÃ¤Â¹ÂÃ¥â€ºÂ­" in s["text"]]
 230:     if len(welcome) != 1:
 231:         raise Fail(f"{label}: welcome spoken {len(welcome)} times (expect 1)")
 232:     last_cancel = None
 233:     for e in log:
 234:         if e["a"] == "cancel":
 235:             last_cancel = e["t"]
 236:         elif e["a"] == "speak" and last_cancel is not None:
 237:             gap = e["t"] - last_cancel
 238:             if gap < 148:
 239:                 raise Fail(f"{label}: speak {gap:.0f}ms after cancel (<150ms): {e['text'][:20]}")
 240:             last_cancel = None
 241:     prev = None
 242:     for s in speaks:
 243:         if prev and s["text"] == prev["text"] and len(s["text"]) > 2 and (s["t"] - prev["t"]) < 3000:
 244:             raise Fail(f"{label}: instruction repeated within 3s: {s['text'][:24]}")
 245:         prev = s
 246:     return len(speaks)
 247: 
 248: def count_words_in(log):
 249:     return [e["text"] for e in log if e["a"] == "speak" and len(e["text"]) == 1 and e["text"] in NUM_CN]

def build():
    out = []
    for cid, desc in CHARS:
        out.append({"file": f"assets/char/{cid}.png", "prompt": f"{desc}. {STYLE_CHAR}", "transparent": True})
    for iid, desc in ITEMS:
        out.append({"file": f"assets/item/{iid}.png", "prompt": f"{desc}. {STYLE_ITEM}", "transparent": True})
    for iid, desc in ISLANDS:
        out.append({"file": f"assets/bg/{iid}.png", "prompt": f"{desc}. {STYLE_ISLAND}", "transparent": True})
    for bid, desc in BGS:
        out.append({"file": f"assets/bg/{bid}.png", "prompt": f"{desc}. {STYLE_BG}", "transparent": False, "size": "1536x1024"})
    return out

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import json
    m = build()
    print(json.dumps([a["file"] for a in m], indent=1))
    print(len(m), "assets")

self.addEventListener('activate', e => {
  e.waitUntil((async () => {
    const keys = await caches.keys();
    await Promise.all(keys.filter(k => k !== CACHE).map(k => caches.delete(k)));
    await self.clients.claim();
  })());
});

self.addEventListener('fetch', e => {
  const req = e.request;
  if (req.method !== 'GET') return;
  const url = new URL(req.url);
  if (url.origin !== location.origin) return;
  const isPage = req.mode === 'navigate' || url.pathname.endsWith('/index.html') || url.pathname.endsWith('/');
  if (isPage) {
    // Ã§Â½â€˜Ã§Â»Å“Ã¤Â¼ËœÃ¥â€¦Ë†Ã¯Â¼Å¡Ã¤Â¿Â® bug Ã¥ÂÅ½Ã§â€Â¨Ã¦Ë†Â·Ã¥Ë†Â·Ã¦â€“Â°Ã¥ÂÂ³Ã¥Â¾â€”Ã¦â€“Â°Ã§â€°Ë†Ã¯Â¼â€ºÃ¦â€“Â­Ã§Â½â€˜Ã¥â€ºÅ¾Ã©â‚¬â‚¬Ã§Â¼â€œÃ¥Â­Ëœ
    e.respondWith((async () => {
      try {
        const r = await fetch(req);
        if (r && r.ok) {
          const copy = r.clone();
          e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(req, copy)));  // Ã¥â€ºÅ¾Ã¥Â¡Â«Ã¥Å“Â¨ worker Ã§â€Å¸Ã¥â€˜Â½Ã¥â€˜Â¨Ã¦Å“Å¸Ã¥â€ â€¦Ã¥Â®Å’Ã¦Ë†Â
        }
        return r;
      } catch (err) {
        const c = await caches.open(CACHE);
        return (await c.match(req)) || (await c.match('./')) || (await c.match('index.html')) || Response.error();
      }
    })());
    return;
  }
  // Ã§Â´Â Ã¦ÂÂÃ§Â¼â€œÃ¥Â­ËœÃ¤Â¼ËœÃ¥â€¦Ë†Ã¯Â¼Å’Ã¦Å“ÂªÃ¥â€˜Â½Ã¤Â¸Â­Ã¥â€ºÅ¾Ã¦ÂºÂÃ¥Â¹Â¶Ã¥â€ºÅ¾Ã¥Â¡Â«
  e.respondWith((async () => {
    const hit = await caches.match(req);
    if (hit) return hit;
    try {
      const r = await fetch(req);
      if (r && r.ok) {
        const copy = r.clone();
        e.waitUntil(caches.open(CACHE).then(c => c.put(req, copy)));
      }
      return r;
    } catch (err) { return Response.error(); }
  })());
});

  55: def contact_sheet(files, out, cols=6, cell=200, label=True):
  56:     from PIL import ImageDraw
  57:     rows = (len(files) + cols - 1) // cols
  58:     sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cols*cell, rows*(cell+18)), (245, 245, 250))
  59:     d = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)

  62:         if not os.path.exists(p): continue

  65:         x = (i % cols)*cell; y = (i // cols)*(cell+18)
  66:         bg = Image.new("RGBA", (cell, cell), (255,255,255,255))
  67:         bg.paste(im, ((cell-im.size[0])//2, (cell-im.size[1])//2), im)
  68:         sheet.paste(bg.convert("RGB"), (x, y))

  70:             d.text((x+6, y+cell+2), os.path.basename(rel).replace(".png",""), fill=(60,60,80))
  71:     sheet.save(os.path.join(ROOT, out), optimize=True)
  72:     print("sheet:", out)

+def sha12(path):
+    h = hashlib.sha1()

 def qa(asset):
     """Ã¨â€¡ÂªÃ¥Å Â¨Ã¦Â£â‚¬Ã¦Å¸Â¥: Ã¥Â°ÂºÃ¥Â¯Â¸ / Ã¥â€ºâ€ºÃ¨Â§â€™alpha / Ã¤Â¸Â»Ã¤Â½â€œbboxÃ¥ÂÂ Ã¦Â¯â€(22%-97%, Ã¤Â¸Å½DESIGN Ã‚Â§9.5Ã¤Â¸â‚¬Ã¨â€¡Â´) /

 def contact_sheet(files, out, cols=6, cell=200, label=True):
     from PIL import ImageDraw
     rows = (len(files) + cols - 1) // cols
     sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cols*cell, rows*(cell+18)), (245, 245, 250))
     d = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
     for i, rel in enumerate(files):
         p = os.path.join(ROOT, rel)
         if not os.path.exists(p): continue
         im = Image.open(p).convert("RGBA")
         im.thumbnail((cell-8, cell-8), Image.LANCZOS)
         x = (i % cols)*cell; y = (i // cols)*(cell+18)
         bg = Image.new("RGBA", (cell, cell), (255,255,255,255))
         bg.paste(im, ((cell-im.size[0])//2, (cell-im.size[1])//2), im)
         sheet.paste(bg.convert("RGB"), (x, y))
         if label:
             d.text((x+6, y+cell+2), os.path.basename(rel).replace(".png",""), fill=(60,60,80))
     sheet.save(os.path.join(ROOT, out), optimize=True)
     print("sheet:", out)

  - flash 指向牌区；compare 使用稳定的获胜侧元素。compare 虽是直接元素而非函数，但目标不会随阶段变化，因此不存在随机或失效指向。[index.html:953](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:953) [index.html:1162](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:1162)

- P1-02：`_speakNow()` 已按 generation 与当前 instruction UID 双闸保护 `_instrActive` 和 duck；计数通道按 generation 保护 `_activeCountSay`。[index.html:438](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:438) `_enqCount()` 也在触碰新队列前退休过期回调。[index.html:524](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:524) `stopAll()` 会推进 generation 并重置全局状态。[index.html:548](/D:/ClaudeCode/kidmath/index.html:548)

   1: # 数字小英雄 🌟
   2: 
   3: **正式地址：https://edliu1105.github.io/number-heroes/**
   4: 
   5: 给 3–4 岁中文小朋友的数学理解启蒙乐园——不是背诵，是真正理解数字、数量和加法。
   6: 
   7: 孙悟空、葫芦娃、复仇者（含蜘蛛侠、迈尔斯、格温）、汪汪队、Bluey、小猪佩奇——39 位小伙伴住在云上六座欢乐岛，等孩子来帮忙"数一数"。
   8: 
   9: ## 教学核心
  10: 
  11: 打通三种表征的连接：**实物数量 ↔ 数词（口语"三"）↔ 数字符号（3）**，再在数量上做操作（合起来/比多少）。
  12: 
  13: 七种活动，对应七项技能，自适应难度（1–3 → 1–5 → 1–10）：
  14: 
  15: | 活动 | 技能 |
  16: |---|---|
  17: | 数一数 | 一一对应 + 基数原则 |
  18: | 找数字 | 数量/数词 → 数字符号 |
  19: | 喂一喂 | 按数取物（生产性任务，主动提交） |
  20: | 闪一闪 | 感数（1–4 一眼识数） |
  21: | 谁的多 | 数量比较 |
  22: | 合起来 | 合成——加法的概念本体 |
  23: | 来了几个 | 加法故事（先数全体） |
  24: 
  25: ## 技术
  26: 
  27: - 单 `index.html` + `assets/`，vanilla JS，零依赖，PWA 离线完整可玩
  28: - 全程中文语音（Web Speech API），针对 iPad WebKit 的完整军规实现（手势解锁/安全打断/事件优先+超时兜底/计数词有序通道/无声检测）
  29: - 全部美术素材由 GPT Image 2 生成（512px 量化优化，总量 ~4.2MB）
  30: - Playwright 八组自动化测试为发布门槛（全流程/语音时间线/慢TTS排空/连点压力/离线整岛/WebKit 横竖屏七活动矩阵/运动命中）
  31: 
  32: ## 本地运行
  33: 
  34: ```bash
  35: python -m http.server 8137
  36: # 打开 http://localhost:8137
  37: ```
  38: 
  39: ## 测试
  40: 
  41: ```bash
  42: python tests/run_tests.py
  43: ```
  44: 
  45: ## 更新方法
  46: 
  47: 改代码 → push 到 main → iPad 上刷新一次页面即生效（页面网络优先策略，素材缓存优先）。
  48: 
  49: ## 家长功能
  50: 
  51: 地图右下角齿轮**长按 3 秒**：学习进度、难度档位、语音诊断（iPad 无声排查）、减少动画、重置。

 119: 
 120: 音效：WebAudio 合成（叮、噗、啵、琶音彩带、温和"嗯？"），零音频文件。循环无声 `<audio>`（军规 5，家长实测经验，保留）用 0.5s/8kHz 极小循环、音量 0.01，页面隐藏时由系统自动暂停，能耗影响可忽略。
 121: 
 122: ## 6. 视觉与素材
 123: 
 124: - 统一美术风格串（每张图复用保证一致性）：*cute chibi cartoon, flat colors with soft cel shading, thick clean dark-brown outlines, big sparkling eyes, friendly smile, sticker style, children's picture-book aesthetic, transparent background, no text, no watermark*。
 125: - 清单：39 角色 + 8 物品（桃子/包子/葫芦/能量星/骨头饼干/网球/曲奇/苹果）+ 6 岛屿 + 7 背景 = 60 张游戏素材, 另有 3 张 PWA 图标（PIL 合成）。
 126: - 全部 codex CLI → GPT Image 2，1024×1024 透明 PNG（背景 1536×1024 或 1024×1024 不透明）；codex 用 magick 验证四角 alpha=0。
 127: - 优化：PIL 缩 512px + 256 色量化（背景 1024px）。目标 assets 总量 < 8MB（离线缓存友好）。
 128: - 版权三层：先近似卡通版 → 被拒/质量差改致敬版（同配色同特征换名）→ 公开部署前向家长确认处理方式。
 129: 
 130: ## 7. 技术架构
